We introduce Synthetic Hospital, an open, fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark that resolves the open sharing and verifiable ground truth barriers. Built entirely from public medical-education material with no protected health information, it comprises 1,268 longitudinal patients and 5,602 encounters, where every diagnosis, finding, and temporal relation is grounded in standard ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and with a complete provenance chain back to its source medical education material. Synthetic Hospital is served through a simulated hospital record system that mirrors real EHR infrastructure (standard interoperability APIs, role-based access and function-calling interface). In a blinded review, physicians distinguished its records from real patient charts at near-chance rates (53\%). Across 10 frontier and open models, none approaches ceiling: the best model reconstructs a patient's longitudinal problem list with a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73, level with the mean of seven physicians on a matched subset but well below the best of them (0.89), and misses roughly half of clinically relevant findings when summarizing a chart. Overall, these results highlight that Synthetic Hospital is a difficult and realistic test of clinical AI performance.

</details>


### [94] [How does Adversarial Influence Scale in Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30028)
*Addison J. Wu,Jasin Cekinmez,Michel Liao,Karthik Narasimhan,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM群体易被少量欺骗者撼动，脆弱性由欺骗者比例而非总人数驱动，需专门防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当部分智能体为恶意或受外部指令欺骗时，多智能体协商性能如何恶化，以及这一脆弱性如何随群体规模和欺骗者占比变化，以评估多智能体方法在实际部署中的安全性。

Method: 构建并模拟若干基于大型语言模型（LLM）的多智能体协商实验，系统性地改变欺骗者比例、欺骗者是否能私下协调以及不同模型组合，测量“defection rate”（最初正确的智能体在最终答案上被说服为错误的频率）并与人类从众实验作对比。

Result: 主要发现包括：defection率与欺骗者占比呈线性关系；LLM智能体在少数欺骗者存在时也会频繁屈从（与人类实验不同，人类通常只在多数误导者时被显著影响）；易受攻击性依赖诚实方模型的类型；令人意外的是，欺骗者的私下协调有时降低了欺骗效果。

Conclusion: 群体中易受欺骗的比例决定了多智能体协商系统的脆弱性：被欺骗者的转向（defection）率随欺骗者比例线性上升，因此单纯增加智能体数量不能抵御攻击。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation can improve performance, but what happens when some agents do not act in good faith? In practice, an agent may be deceptive and work to subvert the group, whether through its own objectives or external instruction. We study how susceptibility to deception scales as groups increase in size and deceivers become more prevalent. It is not the number of agents in the group that matters, but the proportion of deceivers. We observe that the defection rate, how often initially correct agents switch to an incorrect final answer, rises linearly with this proportion. Whereas humans in comparable conformity studies are reliably swayed only when misleading confederates form a majority, LLM agents defect regularly even when deceivers remain a minority. Susceptibility also depends on which models are interacting, especially on the honest agent side. Unexpectedly, allowing deceivers to coordinate privately can make them less effective. Altogether, our results show that adding more agents is therefore not a sufficient defense, because the adversary can simply scale with the group.

</details>


### [95] [NNV3: Expanding Neural Network Verification to New Architectures and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30050)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Samuel Sasaki,Ben Wooding,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Muhammad Usama Zubair,Navid Hashemi,Hongchao Zhang,Waseem Abbas,Ipek Oguz,Meiyi Ma,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NNV3 extends NNV's set-based verification to weight perturbations, volumetric and graph inputs, and adds probabilistic and fairness modes, plus new benchmarks and documentation to broaden applicability and usability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address the growing need for formal verification methods that handle diverse AI architectures and input modalities (e.g., video, graphs), robustness to weight perturbation, and fairness guarantees, while providing usable tooling and reproducible benchmarks.

Method: Builds on set-based reachability (Star-set family) and extends it with three new Star variants (ModelStar, VolumeStar, GraphStar). Adds a conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode for intractable deterministic problems and a FairNNV module for certifying counterfactual and individual fairness over continuous input regions. Integrates these into MATLAB with new benchmarks and tutorials.

Result: Implementation of NNV3 with new Star-set types, probabilistic reachability, fairness certification, and multiple domain benchmarks; demonstrated applicability across malware detection, power systems, medical imaging, time series, and action recognition. Improved documentation and developer guides increase accessibility. Quantitative performance details are not provided in the abstract.

Conclusion: NNV3 represents a maturation of the NNV framework into a broader, more practical verification platform that extends set-based reachability to new data/modalities and problem classes (weight perturbations, video/3D, graphs, fairness, probabilistic reachability) while improving usability via benchmarks and documentation.

Abstract: We present NNV3, the latest version of the Neural Network Verification (NNV) tool, a MATLAB framework for formal verification of deep learning models and learning-enabled cyber-physical systems. Building on the set-based reachability foundation of NNV 1.0 (FFNNs, CNNs, NNCS) and NNV 2.0 (RNNs, SSNNs, neural ODEs), NNV3 introduces new members of the Star-set family: ModelStar for verifying networks under weight perturbation, VolumeStar for video and 3D volumetric inputs, and GraphStar for graph neural networks. A conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode complements sound analysis for problems where deterministic verification is intractable, while FairNNV certifies counterfactual and individual fairness properties over continuous input regions. NNV3 introduces new benchmarks for malware detection, graph-based power-system models, medical imaging, variable-length time series data, and action recognition. NNV3 also incorporates tutorials and developer guides through a unified documentation site. This paper details these major updates, demonstrating NNV's maturation into a comprehensive, robust, and accessible verification tool for a diverse range of AI systems.

</details>


### [96] [SciWalker: Synthesizing Scientific Coding Problems with Operator Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30054)
*Chenxi Li,Wenxuan Zeng,Yun Luo,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Yu Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SciWalker：通过算子链+执行反馈自动合成科学编程题并验证可执行性，生成8178题并显著提升训练后LLM的科学编程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的科学编程训练数据稀缺，人工编写成本高且难以系统覆盖多学科与算法组合，因而需要自动化、可扩展且可验证的任务生成方法。

Method: 构建算子库（结合科学库接口与操作模式）并用算子图组织，随机/受控采样算子链作为工作流提示；以工作流提示引导大模型生成题目描述、参考实现与测试用例；在执行过程中用运行失败或错误反馈迭代修复生成内容；并结合验证与质量审查形成最终高质量任务集。

Result: 用SciWalker合成8178个高质量任务，覆盖5个科学领域和32个子领域；用这些任务对Qwen3.5-9B进行GSPO强化学习后，SciCode子问题准确率从29.3%提升到39.2%，提升9.9个百分点，在代码生成、代码修复和推理基准上均有收益。

Conclusion: SciWalker通过结构化的算子链采样与执行反馈，实现了可扩展的、可执行且具科学依据的代码题合成，从而提高了用于科学编程的大规模模型的训练效用。

Abstract: Improving the scientific coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality training data. However, such data remain scarce because manually authoring realistic problems is costly and time-consuming, while systematically covering diverse scientific domains and algorithmic combinations remains challenging. To address this, we introduce SciWalker, a framework for synthesizing scientific coding problems through operator-chain sampling and execution feedback. The framework combines scientific library interfaces with operation modes to instantiate operators, organizes them into operator graphs, and samples operator chains as computational workflow cues. Guided by these cues, we adopt LLMs to generate scientifically grounded problem statements, reference solutions, and tests, with failed generations iteratively repaired using execution feedback. By combining structured workflow composition with verification and quality review, SciWalker enables scalable task generation while promoting scientific grounding, computational diversity, and executability. Using this framework, we construct 8,178 high-quality problems spanning 5 scientific domains and 32 subdomains. To evaluate their training utility, we conduct reinforcement learning on Qwen3.5-9B using the GSPO algorithm. This training improves SciCode subproblem accuracy by 9.9 percentage points, from 29.3% to 39.2%, with gains across scientific code generation, code repair, and reasoning benchmarks. The code for SciWalker is available at https://github.com/lichenx1/SciWalker.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30063)
*Aditya Cowsik,Kfir Dolev,Michael Y. Li,G. Bruno De Luca,Nourya Cohen,Noah D. Goodman,Yoav Levine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A generator-learner self-play over program-induced byte sequences can bootstrap useful synthetic data: proof-of-concept shows predictable zero-shot transfer scaling, in-context learning, and emergent structures without natural-data pretraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To remove human curation from pretraining data by letting models generate their own training distributions, yielding an effectively unbounded, compute-limited source of training data that adapts to the learner's current capabilities.

Method: Two models are trained from random init: a generator that proposes programs interpreted by a universal Turing machine to produce byte sequences, trained with reinforcement learning to maximize learner error (frontier examples), and a learner that autoregressively predicts those byte sequences trained with cross-entropy. The setup searches over computable data-generating processes inspired by Solomonoff induction.

Result: Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss on natural data scales predictably with self-play compute; models show in-context learning and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training, supporting transfer from synthetic to natural data.

Conclusion: Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data demonstrates a proof-of-concept that a generator-learner self-play loop over programs (via a universal Turing machine) can create an adaptive synthetic-data curriculum improving zero-shot performance on natural tasks without access to natural data.

Abstract: Advances in language modeling have been driven by scaling pretraining on ever more data. Yet, the training data is still largely curated on the model's behalf. A more general approach to pretraining would let the model learn to generate the data most useful for its own improvement. This would provide an effectively unbounded source of training data, limited by compute rather than human knowledge. We introduce Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data, an initial proof-of-concept towards realizing this vision. Our procedure casts synthetic data generation as a search over the space of all computable structure, taking inspiration from Solomonoff induction. Starting from random initialization, two models learn in tandem: a generator proposes programs interpreted by a universal Turing machine, generating byte sequences, while a learner autoregressively predicts these byte sequences. The learner is trained with standard cross-entropy, while the generator is trained with reinforcement learning to produce sequences at the frontier of the learner's capabilities, yielding an adaptive curriculum. A universal Turing machine gives us a search space over all computable data-generating processes, imposing little domain-specific structure, and self-play searches over this space for useful training data. We test whether zero-shot performance on natural data improves predictably with self-play compute; this is a clean test of transfer since neither generator nor learner is trained on natural data. Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss exhibits predictable scaling in compute. The models also exhibit in-context learning, and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training.

</details>


### [98] [PrivDrift: Auditing User-Secret Leakage Under Topic Drift in Active LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30094)
*Luciano Maldonado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrivDrift：一个用于审计对话中秘密在话题漂移后可恢复性的基准，结果表明隐私泄露是持久且与模型与诱导强度高度相关的行为性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在持续交互、共享会话与工具增强场景中，用户在对话中公开的敏感信息是否在话题漂移后仍然可通过后续提示被恢复，从而量化会话级别的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 构建 PrivDrift 基准：1000 个受控多轮对话，包含预设秘密、信息密集的漂移回合和标准化的提取探测；在三种具有扩展上下文窗口的 LLM 上运行，并测量在不同秘密类型与诱导强度下的泄露率。

Result: 在测试的模型与设置下，对话级混合泄露率在 38.7% 到 54.6% 之间，且泄露程度显著依赖于模型、秘密类型和诱导强度；在测试的漂移窗口内，额外的话题漂移并不能可靠地降低泄露率。

Conclusion: 对话式 LLM 在话题漂移后仍会保留并可通过后续提示恢复用户披露的敏感信息，将隐私风险视为持久的行为性失效模式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as persistent assistants in user-facing, shared-session, and tool-augmented settings. When users disclose sensitive information during an active conversation, that information may remain behaviorally recoverable through later prompts even after the dialogue shifts to unrelated topics. We introduce \textbf{PrivDrift}, a benchmark for auditing whether user-disclosed secrets remain recoverable after conversational topic drift and persuasion-based probing. PrivDrift contains 1{,}000 controlled multi-turn dialogues with seeded secrets, content-dense drift turns, and standardized extraction probes. Across three LLMs with extended context windows, dialogue-level hybrid leakage remains substantial, ranging from 38.7\% to 54.6\%, and varies strongly by model, secret type, and persuasion intensity. Within the tested drift window, additional topic drift does not reliably reduce leakage, suggesting that privacy risk in active LLM contexts should be evaluated as a persistent behavioral failure mode rather than only as training-data memorization or immediate jailbreak behavior.

</details>


### [99] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用合成客户与工具输出的假设驱动仿真，可在上线前高效筛选与优化 CX 智能体，仿真评估与生产效果高度一致，并带来实质的线上指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业中，CX 智能体需同时完成意图识别、遵守复杂运营策略并可靠调用工具。人工端到端测试覆盖不足，线上试验风险高且可能损害客户信任，因而需要一种安全、高通量的预发布筛选方法。

Method: 构建面向多步代理式工作流的仿真器（Snowglobe），用合成客户模拟对话并注入模拟工具输出，离线运行大量会话以评估不同模型、推理设置与提示工程配置；在 Nubank 的卡片交付/卡片管理客服机器人线上版本上进行对比验证，并通过多轮 A/B 测试验证仿真引导的选择。

Result: 仿真与生产的版本级二元评估器得分高度相关；仿真驱动的迭代在一次线上 A/B 测试中将事务性 NPS 提升 36.69 点；在 16,000+ 次仿真会话筛选出的模型在后续 A/B 测试中将自助完成率提高 8.82 个百分点且对 tNPS 无显著负面影响；实现了对模型、推理设置与提示的大规模探索。

Conclusion: 基于假设驱动的仿真流水线能在不暴露真实客户的前提下，有效筛选与迭代企业级 CX 智能体，使仿真评估与生产表现高度相关，从而实现显著的生产指标改善。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [100] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge通过生成带有可证唯一解与消融证明的隐藏谜题文档，有效测量模型的直觉与世界重建能力，结果表明此任务能大幅重排排行榜并揭示内容过滤器带来的测评偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准通常直接给出问题，无法测评模型在杂乱文本中发现并解决隐含问题的能力；作者希望衡量模型的推断/整合直觉与从碎片化证据重建世界的能力。

Method: 使用生成器构造信件、收据与日志页等文档，其中埋藏一个经SAT求解器在生成时证明唯一解的问题，并为每个实例提供消融证书以证明每条线索的必要性。通过持续生成保证语料可无限更新。对25个前沿模型在600余实例（共17,400条评分记录）和三种匹配条件下进行比较，主要度量“直觉”（仅给故事时的任务成功率）和次级度量“世界重建”（事实恢复）。

Result: 在直觉指标上出现22倍的性能差距，而事实恢复仅为1.6倍；排名重排明显（事实恢复第二的模型在直觉上却排第十四）；部分模型对是否给出显式问题无差异或在未给问题时表现更好；若将拒绝回答记为失败，会把内容过滤器行为误计为模型能力的体现。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了EnigmaForge，一个通过生成带有隐藏逻辑谜题的文档堆来评估模型“直觉”与世界重建能力的基准；实验证明这一任务会显著重排模型排行榜，并暴露出内容过滤器对测评结果的干扰。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [101] [GRASP: Generating, Revising, and Assessing for Strategic Planning with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30147)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过GenPlan/RevPlan/VerPlan三阶段、隔离上下文与多准则验证，GRASP在复杂与多任务规划上显著提升LLM生成可执行计划的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型随着任务复杂度上升可靠性迅速下降，需要一种既能生成可执行计划又能保持规模化与多任务鲁棒性的方案。

Method: 将规划流程解耦为三类隔离上下文的模块：GenPlan（预编译全局宏级指导）, RevPlan（在局部、隔离窗口中探索替代策略）, VerPlan（基于多准则鉴别器独立评估轨迹）。该框架强调隔离上下文与宏规则正则化，以减少策略干扰与记忆污染。

Result: 在多个数据集上显著优于直接LLM规划器：Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling约+12.4%、ZebraLogic约+30.8%、SciBench Math显著提升；在多任务与交错双任务场景下消除了或大幅降低多任务降级，最多绝对增益达+16.7%；并超越前沿推理模型（如GPT-5-mini）约+14.5%。

Conclusion: GRASP通过模块化、策略感知的多阶段规划显著提升了LLM在复杂任务中生成可执行自然语言计划的可靠性，建立了新的性能基准并在多任务设置下消除了传统规划器的性能崩塌。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) typically exhibit a performance profile where reliability degrades as task complexity increases. We address the challenge of generating high-quality natural language executable plans for complex tasks by introducing $\textbf{GRASP}$, a strategy-aware, multi-stage planning framework. GRASP decouples the planning pipeline across specialized, context-isolated modules: it pre-compiles global macro-guidelines (GenPlan), explores alternative localized strategies within isolated context windows (RevPlan), and independently evaluates trajectories using a multi-criteria discriminator (VerPlan). Empirical evaluations show that GRASP consistently establishes a new state-of-the-art frontier across diverse datasets, yielding substantial accuracy gains over direct LLM planners on Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling ($\sim$12.4$\%$$\uparrow$), ZebraLogic ($\sim$30.8$\%$$\uparrow$), and SciBench Math. Crucially, under multi-task scaling-where standard planners suffer immediate performance collapse-GRASP completely flattens the multi-task degradation penalty. In interleaved dual-task environments, GRASP achieves an absolute accuracy gain of up to 16.7$\%$ over direct LLM planners. Furthermore, by isolating context and enforcing strict macro-regularization, GRASP outperforms frontier reasoning models (such as GPT-5-mini) by a margin of 14.5$\%$.

</details>


### [102] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过先蒸馏再基于组件级搜索感知奖励做 RL，能更有效地优化查询理解并提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态标注监督无法反映 QU 各组件在真实检索流水线中的互动与对最终检索质量的影响，需要一个搜索感知的训练方法以直接优化对下游有益的行为。

Method: 先用教师-学生的监督微调（SFT）训练产生符合 schema 的初始策略；随后对每个查询理解组件分别设计与其运营角色对应的奖励信号，通过与真实搜索引擎的在线交互进行策略优化（组件级 RL），而非仅用单一的端到端奖励。

Result: 在 Roblox 搜索上，组件专属优化不仅提升了各组件的效用指标，还将下游 NDCG@20 相较 SFT 提升 8.9 个点，相较使用单一端到端奖励的训练提升 3.5 个点。

Conclusion: 提出的 distill-then-RL 框架通过先用 teacher-student 的监督微调获得合格的策略初始化，再用与搜索引擎实时交互获得的组件专属奖励进行强化学习，从而显著提升查询理解各组件的实用性并改善下游检索效果。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [103] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于SCA理论的SAGE框架，利用代数稀疏化和双曲嵌入两类结构引导，有效改善长程稀疏奖励推理中的探索与累积偏差并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程稀疏奖励推理下，复杂的分支空间导致模型偏向局部可行但结构不稳的路径，并且小偏差沿深度积累压制稀有奖励，需结构性先验来稳定探索与传播信号。

Method: 提出符号闭合分析（SCA）作为理论镜像，刻画分支结构和稀疏奖励如何在具局部可接受性的推理空间中引入两类偏差；基于此，设计SAGE框架：1) 代数稀疏化——将局部可接受候选投影到按操作符索引的代数子空间以抑制虚假分支；2) 双曲结构引导——将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间以提供稠密的深度方向信号，减缓偏差累积。

Result: 在12个基准和7个模型族上对比多种强基线，SAGE表现稳健优于对手；并在真实长程任务（如Andrews-Curtis）上取得显著提升；代码开源。

Conclusion: SAGE通过注入结构性先验（代数稀疏化和双曲结构引导），在长程稀疏奖励推理任务中同时缓解探索偏差与累积偏差，从而显著提升了模型在多项基准上的性能；在Andrews-Curtis等开放长程问题上能达到高达8倍的改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [104] [ExplorationBench: Measuring AI Systems' Exploration in Verifiable Alien Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30199)
*Ming Zhang,Zhenghao Xiang,Peizhong Gao,Yujiong Shen,Yuhui Wang,Zhonghan Yue,Shihan Dou,Zhangyue Yin,Junjie Ye,Shichun Liu,Weihuang Zheng,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Hongzhang Liu,Jiaqi Shao,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduced ExplorationBench：两个基于可执行“异星”规则的沙箱基准，用于区分真实科学探索与记忆复现。实证表明部分系统能学习新规则，但探索结局对策略敏感且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决评估 AI 科学探索能力时两大挑战：如何验证新假设的真实性（可校验答案），以及如何区分真实的探索发现与仅凭预训练数据回忆的能力。为此需要一个既可精确验证又能排除简单记忆解决方案的测试环境。

Method: 构建两个沙箱（AlienCode，AlienLogic），每个含若干 discovery targets 与任务，提供带缺陷的说明书、任务特定的环境反馈和工具调用接口；通过可执行的“异星”规则保证答案可精确验证且与人类先验冲突；让系统在沙箱中探索以获得规则，然后在留出的任务上评估其是否真正发现并应用新规则。对10个系统进行基准测试，比较学习曲线与不同探索轨迹的效果。

Result: 提供了包含 31/24 个 discovery targets 和共计 140 个任务的基准（分别在两个沙箱中），并在 10 个 AI 系统上进行了评测：结果表明顶尖系统能在一定程度上习得新规则并完成留出任务，但表现依赖于探索策略、初始轨迹与反馈利用，且存在性能波动与回退现象。该基准可揭示系统在长期探索中的稳健性与知识积累能力的局限。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 ExplorationBench 为评估 AI 在“科学探索”能力上的表现提供了可验证、可执行且与现有知识冲突的基准，从而能区分真实探索与预训练记忆。实验显示最强模型可以学习并应用不熟悉的规则，但性能在不同探索轨迹上差异显著，且持续探索可能造成停滞或回退。总体上，ExplorationBench 是向能在未知环境中通过探索获得并运用新知识的 AI 前进的一步。

Abstract: Scientific discovery begins where known problems end. There, AI systems must engage in exploration: framing hypotheses, designing experiments, and iterating on the results. However, evaluating this ability is difficult: (1) how to verify whether a genuinely new hypothesis holds, and (2) how to determine whether a system has discovered it through exploration or merely recalled related knowledge from pre-training data. To this end, we introduce ExplorationBench, which turns the wicked problem of evaluating scientific exploration into a concrete and tractable framework built on verifiable Alien Worlds: their rules are executable, so every answer can be checked exactly, and they conflict with familiar knowledge, so recall alone cannot solve the tasks. The benchmark contains two sandboxes, AlienCode (31 discovery targets, 70 tasks) and AlienLogic (24 discovery targets, 70 tasks). Each sandbox provides a flawed manual, task-specific environmental feedback, and a dedicated tool-call schema. Systems use these resources to explore the sandbox, then solve held-out tasks. We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [105] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BRIE是一种基于自动生成并经临床验证的可持续EHR信息检索评估框架，实验证明当前LLM在跨文档临床信息检索与合成上存在显著短板，强调了持续更新评测的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手工策划的临床评测集维护成本高、易过时，且难以反映快速迭代的模型与临床场景；需要一种可扩展、可持续并能不断刷新以防数据泄露的评估方法来保证在EHR系统中部署的LLM的安全性与实用性。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的基准生成框架：自动从纵向EHR笔记生成问题—答案对；由19位临床专家验证生成器及样本，形成Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE)；使用BRIE对九款LLM和五种推理/检索策略进行评估，并分析模型在不同问题类型（如跨文档合成）上的表现。

Result: 构建并公开BRIE；临床验证通过后BRIE可生成多种答案以反映临床推理差异并支持持续更新；评估结果显示多款现有LLM在信息检索/合成任务上频繁遗漏重要临床信息，尤其是需要跨文档和跨就诊综合推理的问题，从而揭示当前模型和策略的明显不足。

Conclusion: 本文表明：通过自动从纵向EHR病程记录生成问答对并由临床人员验证，可以构建可持续维护的临床检索评估基准（BRIE）；使用该基准评估发现多款主流LLM在检索与合成跨文档、跨就诊信息时常遗漏关键临床信息，证明可扩展基准生成对于在快速演进的医疗环境中对临床LLM进行严格且及时评估是必要的。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [106] [AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30264)
*Jiabin Qiu,Zixuan Chen,Hongye Cao,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce AD-WM: add inverse-dynamics and normalized action-recovery regularizers to latent dynamics so models retain action information, yielding large gains in counterfactual MPC performance and zero-shot transfer.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC must compare alternate candidate action sequences from the same state, so a model that minimizes only factual transition error can still fail to distinguish outcomes of different actions (poor counterfactual discrimination). The goal is to build world models that better separate action-dependent trajectories for counterfactual selection.

Method: AD-WM (Action-Discriminative World Model) uses residual latent dynamics together with predictor-level action-recovery regularization. The regularization comprises an inverse-dynamics head and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information, both encouraging transitions to retain information about the action that caused them. Auxiliary heads are used only during training and discarded at test time; planning uses standard MPC (CEM).

Result: Empirically, AD-WM substantially improves planning success: on OGBench-Cube hard-start success rises from 3.7% to 52.0% versus a matched LeWM baseline; mean success improves in 4/5 simulated environments. Diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not predict closed-loop success, while a CEM-aligned elite regret metric correlates better with success. Zero-shot transfer to a Franka pick-and-place setup (frozen V-JEPA2 encoder and matched DROID post-training) improves basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation.

Conclusion: World models intended for planning must preserve action-dependent differences needed to distinguish counterfactual candidate actions; optimizing only factual prediction accuracy is insufficient. AD-WM demonstrates that training with action-recovery objectives yields much better closed-loop planning performance.

Abstract: Latent world models are typically trained to predict factual transitions, whereas model predictive control (MPC) must compare alternative actions from the same state. A model can therefore achieve low factual prediction error yet poorly distinguish candidate actions. We introduce AD-WM, an action-discriminative joint-embedding world model for counterfactual MPC. AD-WM combines residual latent dynamics with predictor-level action-recovery regularization, using inverse dynamics and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information. Both objectives encourage planning transitions to preserve action information; their auxiliary heads are discarded at test time, leaving MPC unchanged. On OGBench-Cube, AD-WM improves hard-start success from 3.7% to 52.0% over a matched LeWM baseline and improves mean success over the reproduced baseline in four of five simulation environments. Planning diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not follow the closed-loop success ordering, whereas CEM-aligned elite regret tracks success more closely. With a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder and matched DROID post-training, AD-WM also improves zero-shot transfer to our Franka setup, increasing basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation. These results suggest that world models for planning should preserve action-dependent differences needed for counterfactual selection, rather than optimize factual prediction accuracy alone. More videos and code are available at https://ad-wm.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [IaC-Guard-V: A Verification Framework for LLM-Generated Infrastructure-as-Code Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28488)
*Lokesh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IaC-Guard-V 提出四维验证框架并基于 70 个真实案例评估 LLM 修复；结果表明必须用验证引导的迭代策略才能获得高可信度修补，开源模型在配合验证时具备显著成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IaC 误配置是云安全事件的重要成因，尽管 LLM 被提议用于自动修复，但由于扫描器差异、无法通过普通单元测试验证基础设施语义以及供应商规则碎片化，LLM 生成修补的可信度仍然未知，需系统化的验证方法。

Method: 提出 IaC-Guard-V 验证中心框架，从语法有效性、目标问题解决、回归安全性和补丁最小性四个维度评估 AI 生成的 IaC 修补；构建 70 个真实误配置的 Terraform 与 Kubernetes 工件基准，涵盖 70 条扫描器规则和 8 类违规；在三类修复策略（单次修复、结构化提示、验证引导迭代）上对三类 LLM 家族进行 630 次运行评估。

Result: 所有模型在语法有效性上达 100%；单次修复通过全部验证的比率仅 32–50%；采用验证引导的迭代修复后，已验证修复率提升至 68–92%；开源模型在采用验证引导修复时以每个已验证修复约 1/12 的成本超越未采用验证的最强商用模型；商用模型在 Kubernetes 修复上接近完美，而开源模型需迭代验证以达到类似效果；结构化提示持续降低 Terraform 修复质量。

Conclusion: 验证引导的迭代修复是可信自动修复 IaC 的关键：单次输出通常语法正确但无法通过完整验证，迭代并结合验证器显著提升通过率；在成本-效果比上，开源模型配合验证策略能优于未使用验证的商用模型；结构化提示在 Terraform 上反而降低修复质量，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) misconfigurations are a leading cause of cloud security incidents, and Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as automated repair agents. Yet the trustworthiness of LLM-generated IaC repairs remains underexplored. IaC presents distinctive verification challenges: security scanners may behave differently across tools and configurations, infrastructure semantics cannot be validated through ordinary unit tests, and provider-specific rules create a fragmented verification landscape. We present IaC-Guard-V, a verification-centered framework that evaluates AI-generated IaC repairs through four dimensions: syntactic validity, target-issue resolution, regression safety, and patch minimality. We construct a benchmark of 70 real-world misconfigured Terraform and Kubernetes artifacts spanning 70 unique scanner rules and eight violation classes, and evaluate three repair strategies across three LLM families in 630 runs. Although all models achieve 100% syntactic validity, only 32-50% of single-shot repairs pass full verification. Verification-guided iterative repair significantly improves verified-fix rates to 68-92%. An open-source model with verification-guided repair outperforms the strongest commercial model without verification at one-twelfth the cost per verified fix. Kubernetes repairs reach near-perfect rates for commercial models but require verification-guided iteration for the open-source model. Structured prompting consistently reduces Terraform repair quality, challenging common assumptions about constrained LLM output. The benchmark and artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [108] [Pretraining and adapting a language model on a dependency-free stack: GPT-2 124M from random weights, reproduced against llm.c, and a clinical adapter for Qwen3-0.6B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28568)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者用独立实现numbat完成了GPT-2级别模型的预训练与临床适配，结果与既有实现高度接近且吞吐率更高，支持训练结论对实现的鲁棒性，但仍需更多公开细节和多样化验证来完全排除实现依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前几乎所有在用语言模型都由一类软件训练，难以区分训练结论是关于模型本身还是关于那套软件，需第二实现来验证训练结论是否与实现无关。

Method: 使用numbat（用Zig实现、无第三方运行时依赖）作为机器学习栈，从随机初始化预训练一个124.4M参数的GPT-2模型，训练语料约9.91B标记；随后在独立小模型上进行临床问答适配。全流程在相同硬件上运行参考实现并由有权中止运行的sidecar监督。比较了交叉熵、HellaSwag等基准并测量吞吐率，与PyTorch参考实现对比。

Result: 预训练后held-out交叉熵3.2588（已发布值3.29）；HellaSwag 0.3053（已发布0.299）；在8组配对评估中与同机参考实现的差异平均为0.0608，重跑参考实现换硬件带来的差异为0.0035；吞吐率numbat 43,374 tok/s vs PyTorch 41,202 tok/s，三卡扩展2.769x；临床适配held-out loss 2.1899 vs 2.1941。注：模型非医疗器械，未做临床验证。

Conclusion: 作者成功展示了用不同软件栈完成从预训练到下游适配的完整生命周期，实现了与既有实现高度接近的性能，从而支持“性能更多源于模型和训练流程而非单一软件实现”的论点。

Abstract: Almost every language model in service was trained by one family of software. That concentration makes a question hard to settle: how much of what is known about training a language model describes language models, and how much describes that software? Settling it needs a second implementation able to carry a model through a whole lifecycle rather than reproduce one operator.
  We report such a lifecycle. Using numbat, a machine-learning stack written in Zig with no third-party runtime dependencies, we pretrain a 124.4 M-parameter GPT-2 from random initialisation over 9.91 B tokens of web text, then adapt a separate small model to clinical question answering. A reference implementation runs on identical hardware at both stages, and a sidecar with authority to halt a run supervises each.
  Agreement is close. Held-out cross-entropy finishes at 3.2588 against a published 3.29, and HellaSwag at 0.3053 against 0.299; across 8 paired evaluations it sits below a same-machine reference at every point, by 0.0608 on average. Re-running that reference on different hardware moves it 0.0035, which bounds how much of any gap is method rather than framework. Throughput does not pay for agreement: measured in one session at a production configuration, numbat reaches 43,374 tokens per second against PyTorch's 41,202, scaling 2.769x over three cards. Clinical adaptation ends at 2.1899 held-out loss against 2.1941.
  Neither model is a medical device, and neither is validated for clinical use.

</details>


### [109] [Change-Provenant Supervision: Governing Learned Artifacts Under Policy Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28574)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Separate recorded lineage from independent revalidation; use byte-reconstruction and a sealed ledger to detect provenance-stale/adulterated learned artifacts. Transitive-lineage nomination narrows impact scope compared to document-wide invalidation and, together with per-target revalidation, gives sound admission decisions; further work needed on scalability, attacker models and operational integration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Learned artifacts may pass output tests despite provenance staleness or omitted dependency edges; governance needs a sound way to admit or refuse artifacts after authority changes without assuming the recorded graph is complete.

Method: Propose a design that separates recorded lineage from independent current-contract revalidation; implement two evidence layers (byte-derived reconstruction and a sealed current-contract ledger) and evaluate with LoRA packages for Qwen3-14B and a controlled amendment harness; compare transitive lineage nomination to document-wide invalidation and test the effect of omitting load-bearing edges.

Result: Stale and full-fresh adapters produced identical plans on 80 authority-neutral inputs, yet reconstruction + sealed ledger refused stale/naive-append and admitted full-fresh/lineage-selective packages. In a 6-amendment controlled test, transitive lineage recovered all 40 semantically invalidated amendment-target pairs while nominating 56 candidates (vs 120 under document-wide invalidation); all 56 had declared paths to amended clauses, though 16 remained content-valid. Omitting one load-bearing edge per amendment reduced semantic recall to 34/40 and independent revalidation refused all six affected packages. Authors stress results characterize governance properties, not operational metrics or cost savings.

Conclusion: Recorded dependency graphs alone cannot guarantee absence of omitted edges; separating recorded lineage (proposed impact scope and dependency explanation) from independent per-target current-contract revalidation supplies sound admission decisions after authority changes.

Abstract: A recorded dependency graph cannot certify that it contains no omitted edge. For learned artifacts, graph-scoped invalidation therefore cannot by itself justify admission after authority changes, especially when output testing misses a provenance-stale derivation. We separate recorded lineage, which proposes impact scope and a dependency explanation, from independent current-contract revalidation of every retained target, which supplies soundness relative to the declared contract.
  We evaluate this design in two evidence layers. Four Qwen3-14B LoRA packages bind 1.028 GB of adapter bytes to training and authority records. Stale and full-fresh adapters trained on mutually exclusive versioned supervision emitted identical plans on all 80 tested authority-neutral inputs. Byte-derived reconstruction and a sealed current-contract ledger nevertheless refused the stale and naive-append packages and admitted full-fresh and lineage-selective packages. In a controlled harness with six role-separated amendments, transitive lineage recovered all 40 semantically invalidated amendment-target pairs while nominating 56 impact candidates, compared with 120 under document-wide invalidation. All 56 had a declared path to an amended clause, although 16 remained content-valid. Omitting one load-bearing edge per amendment reduced semantic recall to 34/40 while independent revalidation refused all six affected packages.
  The results establish properties of a controlled governance mechanism, not an enterprise invalidation rate, unlearning result, operationally optimal workflow, or measured total-cost reduction.

</details>


### [110] [Developing a Unified Verification and Validation Activity Standard at JPL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28600)
*Maximilian Vierlboeck,Ellen Van Wyk,Natalia Sanchez,Bogdan Oaida,Torrance James Eberhart,Marie Piette Gomez,Christopher Delp,Marijke G. Jorritsma,Alex Desharnais*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过人本研讨与SysML关系化建模，提出并在Jama平台验证了一套统一的V&V活动模式，旨在减少JPL内部碎片化并启用可复用、可自动化的V&V流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年JPL的V&V实践出现分歧，导致管理开销增加、跨项目复用率下降与机构知识传递受阻，亟需统一的、既能保障严谨性又具敏捷性的V&V表示与实施方式。

Method: 通过以人为中心的设计研讨会（29名来自不同任务类型与学科的从业者）收集需求与模式，基于关系型架构构建模型，将方法（测试、分析、检查、演示、设计评审）抽象分离，并用平台无关的SysML建模表示需求、V&V活动、场所与证据之间的双向关系；随后在JPL的Jama平台上以模板与模块化项类型实现。

Result: 形成了一个关系化、可复用且平台无关的V&V活动模式（SysML表示），并在Jama中示范性实现，支持受控定制、模板化、模式复用、自动化与数字线程集成，从而在实践中平衡严谨性与敏捷性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一V&V活动模式化架构能减少JPL内部因实践分歧带来的碎片化，提升跨项目效率与知识传承，同时在保持方法分离的前提下维护共同属性集，证明了在实践平台（Jama）中可控定制与自动化的可行性。

Abstract: Verification and validation practices (V&V) at NASA's Jet Propulsion Laboratory (JPL) have diverged over the past decade, creating fragmentation that increases overhead, reduces cross-project efficiencies, and inhibits institutional knowledge transfer. We present a unified V&V activity schema developed through human-centered design workshops involving 29 practitioners across multiple mission types and disciplines. The schema builds on a relationship-based architecture that allows for separating methods (Test, Analysis, Inspection, Demonstration, and Review of Design) while maintaining a common attribute set. Formalized as a platform-agnostic SysML model, the schema defines bidirectional relationships between requirements, V&V activities, venues, and evidence. Implementation in JPL's Jama platform demonstrates controlled customization through templates and modular item types, balancing rigor with agility while enabling automations, pattern reuse, and digital thread integration.

</details>


### [111] [Soundness Checking of Taint Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28750)
*Samarth Kishor,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Use an LLM to guess precise taint models and a symbolic must-not-flow checker (backed by lightweight static analyses and recursive callee-model inference) to prove soundness; this scales and succeeds for the majority (93%) of tested Go methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manual taint-modeling is tedious and error-prone; full inter-procedural automatic modeling is often inefficient. The goal is to obtain precise, sound taint models scalably by leveraging LLMs while ensuring soundness via verification.

Method: A guess-and-check pipeline: (1) an LLM agent synthesizes a precise taint-flow model for a library method; (2) a symbolic algorithm computes which taint flows must not occur for the model to be sound; (3) lightweight static analyses (type system, pointer analysis) attempt to prove those must-not-flows; (4) when needed, the algorithm infers maximally-general callee models and recursively verifies them, avoiding expensive full inter-procedural taint analysis in most cases.

Result: Evaluated on 97 LLM-generated models across methods from 6 large Go codebases: 93% of the covered methods had models proven sound. Proven-sound models were precise and introduced no additional false positives when used to prove taint-flow properties.

Conclusion: Combining LLM-generated taint-flow models with a symbolic "must-not-flow" verification yields a practical, scalable pipeline: most LLM models can be automatically proven sound without full inter-procedural analysis, giving precise models that avoid new false positives.

Abstract: Existing state-of-the-art static taint flow analyses for imperative programming languages can scale to large applications by using precise user-provided taint flow models of library methods. However, manually and precisely modeling a method's taint flows is tedious and potentially unsound. Furthermore, automatically modeling the method via an inter- procedural taint analysis can be inefficient. To solve this problem, we propose a guess-and-check approach: (1) an LLM agent that generates a precise taint flow model of a method and (2) a symbolic algorithm to check the soundness of the model. The algorithm deduces which taint flows must not occur in the method for the LLM's taint flow model to be sound, and uses lightweight static analyses (e.g., type system and pointer analysis) to prove these must-not-flows. When these analyses are insufficient, the algorithm deduces maximally-general callee models and recursively verifies their soundness, avoiding a full inter-procedural taint analysis in most cases. Since a more precise model requires fewer must-not-flows to be verified, the precision of the LLM's model directly determines the efficiency of our approach. We evaluate our approach on 97 LLM-generated taint flow models for methods in 6 large Go codebases and prove the models sound for 93% of the methods they cover. The proven-sound LLM-generated models are also precise, resulting in no new false-positives when proving taint flow properties.

</details>


### [112] [Category-Based MLM: Unifying Powertypes with Superclasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28810)
*Mira Balaban,Azzam Maraee,Arnon Sturm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CatMLM通过将类别类拆成不参与/参与深度刻画的特征并赋予双重面向，提供了可量化的分层判据和实例/子类决策规则，化解了instance-of非传递性与deep characterization的语义冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLM以哲学与本体论论证重复类型分类的必要性，但在instance-of非传递性与类别对下层影响（deep characterization）之间存在语义冲突且缺乏可操作的分层判据；需要一个既能保留MLM深刻表达力又能提供清晰可量化分层决策的模型。

Method: 在概念层面定义clabject与类别类的语义，明确instance-of的非传递含义并引入类别特征（不参与深度刻画）与对象特征（参与深度刻画）的区分；基于该区分构造双面向类别类，导出可量化的分层准则，并用分析性论证与示例说明子类与instance-of选择的决策规则及MLM与OO的得失。

Result: 提出CatMLM作为一套清晰可操作的MLM规范：区分类别/对象特征、定义类别类的双重facet、给出量化的分层标准与决策规则，并讨论了MLM相较于传统OO的优劣与适用情景，为MLM工程化与工具支持奠定理论基础。

Conclusion: CatMLM提出了一个基于“类别（category）”与“对象（object）”特征区分的多层次建模（MLM）形式化模型，通过赋予类别类（category classes）双重面向（超类facet与powertype facet），解决了instance-of非传递性与深度特征刻画（deep characterization）之间的矛盾，从而给出可量化的分层判据及子类/实例关系的决策规则，同时比较了MLM与传统面向对象建模的权衡。

Abstract: MultiLevel software Modeling (MLM) suggests that conceptual modeling in broad subject domains might require abstraction of multiple classification levels. The MLM approach relies on philosophical arguments, claiming that faithful modeling of real-world domains involves repeated type classification as in ontologies of natural kinds. MLM leveled architecture is interwoven and defined by instance-of interlevel relationships between clabject classes in lower levels to classes termed category classes, in upper levels. The instance-of relation denotes membership of clabjects as type objects in their (powertypes) category classes, and is not transitive.
  All MLM approaches support forms of deep characterization, i.e., category classes can influence classes in lower levels. Deep characterization is an essential feature of superclasses and contradicts the non-transitive membership meaning of instance-of.
  In this paper, we introduce the Category-Based MLM (CatMLM) model, in which category classes have dual superclass and powertype facets, based on the distinction between category features that do not participate in deep characterization, and object features that do. This distinction clarifies the role of levels, provides a clear quantifiable criterion for leveling, and yields a decision rule between the subclass and instance-of relations.
  The contribution of this paper is to introduce a well-defined MLM model that (1) is based on simple, quantifiable level decisions; (2) clarifies how leveling emerges from domain needs; and (3) analyzes gains and losses of MLM vs. plain OO modeling.

</details>


### [113] [Specification-Driven Benchmarking for Automated Program Repair From Static Corpora to Executable Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28896)
*Yasser Ebrahim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出规范驱动的APR基准构建范式：用可执行规范+生成管道替代静态语料，提高实验可控性与可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前APR基准为静态数据集，受限于语料固有特性、易受污染、难以更新与重现，因而需要一个可声明、可生成、可验证并可适应评价需求变化的基准构建方法。

Method: 提出规范驱动的基准范式：定义基准规范维度分类，并将每个维度映射到确定性的架构职责，设计生成管道（独立生成、独立验证、语料管理），并通过端到端示例说明规范选择如何在管道中实现。

Result: 构建了概念框架与维度分类，阐明了独立验证的结构必要性，并通过示例演示规范到实例的可验证生成；尚未提供完整的大规模实现与实证比较。

Conclusion: 将基准视为可执行规范，从而将基准构建从静态语料库的人工策划转向声明式的实验设计，提高可控性、可验证性和可再生性。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) benchmarks have traditionally been constructed as static datasets whose characteristics are inherited from the defects they contain. While this paradigm has enabled decades of progress, finite corpora provide limited experimental control, become increasingly susceptible to contamination as they are reused, and cannot be systematically regenerated or adapted as evaluation requirements evolve. We propose specification-driven benchmarking, a paradigm in which benchmarks are defined by executable specifications and realized through benchmark generation. The specification explicitly declares the intended properties of the benchmark (including program context, fault taxonomy, difficulty, validation strategy, and corpus constraints) while a generation pipeline realizes those requirements through independent generation, validation, and corpus management components. We develop the conceptual foundations of this approach by introducing a taxonomy of benchmark specification dimensions, establishing how each specification dimension maps to deterministic architectural responsibilities, and arguing that independent validation is a structural requirement for trustworthy benchmark generation. An end-to-end example illustrates how specification choices propagate through the pipeline to produce benchmark instances whose properties are independently verifiable. By treating the benchmark as an executable specification rather than a static dataset, the proposed paradigm shifts benchmark construction from artifact curation to declarative experimental design.

</details>


### [114] [Automatic Harness Evolution for Hardware Design Verification: Can LLMs Consolidate Gains Across Discovered Harnesses?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28908)
*Kidus Seyoum,Ajay Mittur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 自动演化能产生强烈的专用改进（完成率和覆盖率显著上升），但正确性和可复现性提升有限；收益多为任务间交换式局部专长，因此应使用存档/多候选选择以保存互补成果并进一步研究如何把这些专长整合成更通用的harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否能自动改进其外部harness，从而提升针对硬件设计/验证类任务的自动化性能与故障定位能力；解决现有harness依赖性强且改进困难的问题。

Method: 在固定的语言模型（subject model）外包一层harness，对12个专有设计-验证（root-cause localization）任务进行自动演化。每个任务进行5次试验，度量包括完成尝试数、any-hit任务覆盖率、以及总正确尝试。还包含一个CVDP跨基准案例研究，比较定义宽度(repair harness)与142任务参考基线的功能通过率。

Result: 自动演化harness在完成尝试数上提升71–76%，在any-hit任务覆盖率上提升80–100%，但总正确尝试仅提升18–24%。可重复性差：仅一项改进在至少两次重复中可复现；候选体之间的增益在任务间会互换而非保留。一个在4任务验证集上有改善的候选体，在包含全部12任务的回放中与基线持平，说明所选增益未跨全池持久。不同候选体呈现有用的搜索、证据和最终化行为，但未能整合为对所有任务占优的单一harness。CVDP案例中，自动演化的定义宽度修复harness相比142任务基线，功能通过率提高35.6%；在修复过程中用于打分的最终功能验证器未暴露给主体代理。总体上结果支持在演化产生互补专长时采用存档感知（archive-aware）选择策略。

Conclusion: 自动演化的harness能在特定硬件设计验证子任务上产生显著的专用改进，但难以合并为一个对所有任务和评价指标都有鲁棒优势的通用harness。需采用存档/多候选体选择策略以利用互补专长。

Abstract: Agent behavior depends on the harness surrounding a language model, but it remains unclear whether language models can reliably improve such harnesses for hardware-design tasks. We study automatic harness evolution around a fixed subject model on 12 proprietary design-verification root-cause localization tasks. Across five trials per task, automatically evolved harnesses increased completed attempts by 71-76% and any-hit task coverage by 80-100%, while total correct attempts improved by only 18-24%. The strongest success reproducible at least twice result improved by one task, and later candidates exchanged gains across tasks rather than preserving them. An auxiliary candidate improved on a four-task validation set excluded from search but tied its baseline on a subsequent 12-task replay containing both search and validation tasks, so the selected gain did not persist across the full pool. Across the tested lineage, useful search, evidence, and finalization behaviors appeared in different candidates but did not consistently consolidate into a single harness that dominated across tasks and metrics. In a separate CVDP cross-benchmark case study, an automatically evolved defined-width repair harness produced 35.6% more functional passes than its 142-task reference baseline; the final functional verifier scored completed outputs but was not shown to the subject agent during repair. These results support archive-aware selection when evolution yields complementary specializations without consistent consolidation.

</details>


### [115] [Human-AI Collaboration for Multi-Line Task Adjustment Using Local Large Language Models and a Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29061)
*Teng-Hsien Ko,Chin-Te Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个基于本地LLM+数字孪生+人工决策的Propose-Verify-Decide多线策略调整与可追溯工作流，在虚拟测试中展示出分阶段筛查的有效性与高仿真验证通过率，但需更多多样化、长期与物理层面的验证来证明稳健性与可推广性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化系统需在任务、设备状态与人员条件变化下自适应，同时为人工复核提供可证据化的决策路径，因此需要一个能提出、验证并保留审计痕迹的策略生成与审查机制。

Method: 构建多线任务调整系统，将本地大型语言模型、数字孪生与人工决策整合入Propose-Verify-Decide流程：将操作员意图映射为结构化需求，生成有界候选策略集合，并通过语义检查、仿真执行与运维约束验证；使用链式记录保留请求->验证证据->决策的可追溯性。评估使用30条固定测试记录，基于四条虚拟手术器械分拣线进行功能与模型生成测试。

Result: 30个固定测试记录中：18例符合工作流期望；自动生成并通过工作流的成功率为3/10；对无效输入的正确拒绝为7/8；所有通过先前检查并到达工程复核（CP6）的4例均通过复核；8条仿真证据记录的放置验证平均通过率97.50%；首个可审查响应平均耗时12.94秒，仿真验证平均耗时164.39秒；未通过案例主要由于语义扭曲、证据不完整与漏判无效输入。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种可追溯的策略-审查工作流：分阶段筛查与确认在所测虚拟设置中有效，但并未证明系统的整体可靠性或长期稳定性，需更广泛的测试与物理验证。

Abstract: Automation systems must adapt to changing tasks, equipment states, and staffing conditions while providing evidence for human review. This study presents a multi-line task-adjustment system integrating a local large language model, a digital twin, and human decision-making. A Propose-Verify-Decide workflow translates operator intent into structured requirements, generates a bounded set of candidate strategies, and checks semantics, simulation execution, and operational constraints. Linked records preserve traceability from requests to verification evidence and decisions. Thirty fixed test records were evaluated using four virtual surgical-instrument sorting lines: 28 assessed the workflow and two assessed model generation. Eighteen workflow cases met expectations; autonomous strategy-workflow success was 3/10, and correct rejection of invalid inputs was 7/8. All four cases that passed preceding checks, produced complete evidence, and reached final engineering review (CP6) passed that review. Together with the correct blocking of strategies that failed throughput constraints, this supports the effectiveness of staged screening and confirmation within the tested setting. Mean placement-validation pass rate across eight simulation evidence records was 97.50%. Mean times to the first reviewable response and simulation verification, excluding startup, were 12.94 and 164.39 s, respectively. Remaining failures involved semantic distortion, incomplete evidence, and missed invalid inputs. The results demonstrate a traceable strategy-review workflow, but do not establish overall reliability or long-term stability. Broader testing and physical evaluation are needed to assess generalizability.

</details>


### [116] [Design, development, and preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29068)
*Albert D. Ritzhaupt,Jhonathan Sora-Cardenas,Priya Ganapathy Prasad,Kai A. Hackney,Neha Rani,Pedro Guillermo Feijóo-García*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建并初步验证了SESES量表，EFA支持五因子结构（需求工程、团队协作、质量管理、设计与架构、敏捷方法），样本量充足、解释方差约57%，下一步应补充CFA、更多效度证据和测量不变性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为软件工程领域提供一个领域特定的自我效能测量工具，以便更精确地评估本科生在需求工程、团队与协作、质量管理、设计与架构、敏捷方法论等核心能力的自我效能感。

Method: 基于软件工程教学框架与自我效能理论构建项目池(n=87)，对527名修过软件工程课程的本科生施测，采用主轴因子(Principal Axis Factoring)和Promax斜交旋转的探索性因子分析(EFA)提取因子。

Result: EFA结果支持原始理论框架的五维结构，条目呈简单结构、交叉载荷最小，五因子合计解释约57%方差，且各因子内部一致性良好（文中称“内部一致”但未报具体系数）。

Conclusion: 研究设计合理且初步证据充分，表明软件工程自我效能(SESES)为一个由五个相关但可区分的潜在因子组成的多维构念。

Abstract: The purpose of this research is to design, develop, implement, and provide preliminary validity and reliability evidence of the Software Engineering Self-Efficacy Scale (SESES). Framed by a conceptual framework using guidance in software engineering curriculum and concepts along with the notion of self-efficacy, we generated an initial item pool of n = 87 items to operationalize and measure software engineering self-efficacy among undergraduate computing students. The conceptual framework traces five dimensions: 1) Requirements Engineering, 2) Teamwork and Collaboration, 3) Software Quality Management, 4) Software Design and Architecture, and 5) Software Agile Methodologies. We pilot tested the SESES with n = 527 undergraduate computing students who had completed a software engineering course in the current semester or a previous academic semester. We employed Exploratory Factor Analysis (EFA) with the Principal Axis Factoring method and an oblique (Promax) rotation to examine the underlying structure of the SESES, resulting in the same five internally consistent latent constructs in the conceptual framework with minimal cross-loading and a simple structure in the pattern matrix, explaining approximately 57% of the variability in these data. Our findings suggest that software engineering self-efficacy is a multidimensional construct of five theorized and correlated, yet distinct latent factors. We unpack the limitations and delimitations of the research while exploring undergraduate computing students' software engineering self-efficacy using necessary domain-specific measurements.

</details>


### [117] [VidTutorAssistant: Automating Responses to Programming Tutorial Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29129)
*Ahmad Tayeb,Sonia Haiduc,Mohammad D. Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于RAG和GPT-4的VidTutorAssistant，用于自动回答编程教程视频评论问题；在小规模人工验证中表现显著优于原视频作者的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YouTube编程教学视频评论区有大量观众提问，内容创作者难以及时回复，导致学习者得不到必要澄清；因此希望用自动化系统补充/替代人工回复。

Method: 构建检索增强生成（RAG）流水线：提取视频转录文本、分段并嵌入；对评论进行问题/非问题分类；对问题使用余弦相似度检索最相关转录片段；以检索到的片段作为上下文，调用GPT-4生成答案。

Result: 在从7,522个Python和Java教程中提取的105,553条评论中，抽样440条进行验证。系统在编程语言识别上达0.99准确率、问题分类0.96准确率；对问题的回答在“正确性”和“完整性”上分别达98%和99.5%，优于原作者的89%和90%。

Conclusion: VidTutorAssistant能在自动识别视频编程语言、判定评论是否为问题并基于视频文本检索为观众问题生成高质量回答方面表现优异，且在受控示例上超越了视频作者的回复质量。

Abstract: Programming tutorial videos on YouTube are an important information resource for software developers and students, and their comment sections have evolved into active spaces where viewers ask follow-up questions. The volume of these questions, however, often exceeds what content creators can address, leaving learners without the clarifications they need. We present VidTutorAssistant, a web platform that automates responses to viewer questions on programming video tutorials. VidTutorAssistant implements a retrieval-augmented generation pipeline that extracts a video's transcript, then segments it and embeds it. It then classifies each viewer comment as being a question or non-question, retrieves the most relevant transcript segments to each identified question via cosine similarity, and then generates an answer to the question using an LLM (GPT-4), while grounding the response using the retrieved transcript segments as context. We validate VidTutorAssistant through a study on a subset of 440 user comments selected from a larger dataset of 105,553 comments extracted from 7,522 Python and Java tutorials. VidTutorAssistant is evaluated on various criteria: a) its ability to identify the programming language in a video, achieving a 0.99 accuracy; b) its ability to classify comments into questions and non-questions, reaching a 0.96 accuracy; and c) its ability to produce correct and complete answers to questions, producing 98% correct and 99.5% complete responses, compared with 89% and 90% for the original creators' answers.

</details>


### [118] [Orchestrating AI-Assisted Code Remediation: Socio-Technical Bottlenecks in a Large Industrial Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29172)
*Andreas Bexell,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma söderberg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI 可迅速放大机械性修复能力，但随之转移的瓶颈是 CI 容量、审查成本与变更编排。需要将语义修复集合作为工作单元，并在提交/审查/CI 层面上设计差异化切片与限流机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期且大型代码库中的代码退化修复人工成本高昂。大型语言模型(LLM) 驱动的编码助手能在规模上执行机械性修复，但其对工业级工作流（如 CI、代码审查、团队协调）的影响尚未充分研究。本研究旨在探究当源代码编辑变得廉价时出现的社会技术瓶颈。

Method: 单案例现场研究（为期15天）：一名有经验的开发者使用命令行 AI 编码助手在一个闭源大型 C++ 代码库中修复大规模问题。研究通过三角验证整合Gerrit元数据、开发者日记和团队聊天记录，采用描述性统计与定性编码分析。

Result: AI 辅助修复在短时间内产生数百个提交、数千行变更，导致 CI 与审查注意力饱和。按文件逐次提交会压垮基于提交触发的构建；改为按目录批量并限制每次变更的文件数能恢复吞吐，但仍需主动邀审、就可接受的提交粒度达成协议，并通过迭代跟进解决构建和静态分析失败。

Conclusion: 当机械化编辑变得廉价时，持续集成(CI)、代码审查以及变更协调成为主要瓶颈。要在超大规模代码库中可持续地实施 AI 辅助修复，需要对提交、审查与 CI 批次的粒度进行刻意控制，并将“按语义的变更集合”（例如“修复所有 X 警告”）作为一级工作单元，以便为开发者、审查者和 CI 提供不同的切片策略。

Abstract: Background: Code degradation in large, long-lived codebases is costly to remediate through manual refactoring and opportunistic clean-ups. LLM-based coding assistants can perform mechanical remediation at scale, but their impact on industrial workflows is underexplored. Objective: We investigate how massive AI-assisted code remediation affects build-on-commit continuous integration (CI), code review, and team coordination in a large industrial repository, and which socio-technical bottlenecks constrain such remediation when source editing becomes cheap through AI assistance. Method: We report on a 15-day exploratory single-case field study in which an experienced developer used a command-line AI coding buddy to remediate widespread issues in a closed-source industrial C++ repository. We triangulate Gerrit metadata with a developer diary and team chat, analyzed through descriptive statistics and qualitative coding. Results: AI-assisted remediation rapidly generated hundreds of commits touching thousands of lines, saturating CI and reviewer attention. Naïve per-file commits overloaded build-on-commit CI; Switching to directory-based batching and capping the number of files per change restored throughput, but still required explicit review solicitation, negotiation of acceptable commit granularity, and iterative follow-up to resolve build and static-analysis failures. Conclusion: When mechanical editing is cheap, CI capacity, review effort, and change orchestration become primary bottlenecks. Sustainable AI-assisted remediation in very large repositories requires deliberate control of commit, review, and CI batch granularity and treating semantic change sets, such as ``fix all instances of warning X'', as first-class units of work that can be sliced differently for developers, reviewers, and CI.

</details>


### [119] [HistoRAG: A Citation-Grounded Question Answering Assistant for Teaching with Scanned Local History and Heritage Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29184)
*Hongzhou Duan,Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HistoRAG combines VLM transcription with three specialized stores and a question router to produce page-cited, low-cost QA over scanned regional heritage collections, and shows empirical gains on a 516-question benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teachers need verifiable, source-cited answers when working with hard-to-use local-history primary sources (scanned books without text layers) and supporting catalogs in spreadsheets; generic chatbots are fluent but lack traceable provenance.

Method: Transcribe scanned pages with a vision-language model producing token/line-level confidences; construct three parallel stores (hybrid text index, relational catalog DB, knowledge graph from entity-dense passages); route questions to appropriate store(s); return answers with volume+page citations; evaluate on a 516-question benchmark over 36 volumes and catalogs.

Result: HistoRAG answers more questions correctly than passage retrieval and a graph-based retrieval system, at substantially lower cost per question; it enables teachers to verify claims by inspecting the cited page via a chat interface.

Conclusion: HistoRAG provides verifiable, page-cited question answering over a regional scanned collection and associated catalogs, outperforming standard passage-retrieval and graph-based baselines while reducing per-question cost.

Abstract: Teachers who prepare lessons on local history and cultural heritage work from material that is hard to use. The primary sources are scanned books without a text layer, and the supporting records are administrative catalogs released as spreadsheets. A general chatbot answers such questions fluently but without a verifiable source, which is the property a teacher needs most. This paper presents HistoRAG, a question answering assistant that answers from one regional collection and cites a volume and a page for every fact. HistoRAG transcribes each page with a vision language model and keeps a line level confidence from the token probabilities. It builds three stores from the same collection: a hybrid text index, a relational catalog database, and a knowledge graph extracted only from entity dense passages. A lightweight router sends each question to the stores it needs, so that counting questions reach the database and relational questions reach the graph. We build a benchmark of 516 questions over a collection of 36 scanned volumes and the heritage catalogs of the same region, covering statistical, factual, temporal, and multi-hop questions. HistoRAG answers more questions correctly than passage retrieval baselines and than a graph based retrieval system, at a far smaller cost per question. The assistant runs behind a chat interface, so a teacher can check any statement against the page it came from.

</details>


### [120] [On the Impact of Requirement Smells in LLM-Based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29208)
*Hugo Villamizar,Jannik Fischbach,Mert Şahin,Julian Frattini,Alessio Ferrari,Alexander Korn,Andreas Vogelsang,Daniel Mendez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过将语义/句法/词汇异味注入需求并用系统测试评估LLM生成代码，研究发现异味密度越高，功能正确性越低；不同异味类别影响相似，但高质量需求也不能完全保证正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作显示需求异味会影响自动化需求—代码可追溯性，但缺乏对LLM辅助代码生成中异味影响的实证证据；希望填补该空白并比较异味在不同软件工程任务上的效应差异。

Method: 在之前可追溯性研究的基础上复用数据集与需求异味分类，构建含四个应用的基准集及其系统测试；将语义、句法与词汇异味逐步注入到本为清晰的需求中；使用LLM生成实现并通过系统测试评估功能正确性，分析异味类型与密度对测试通过率的影响。

Result: 总体上，随着异味密度上升，测试通过率下降；不同异味类别表现出相似的负面影响；即使没有显著异味，部分生成代码仍会失败；与先前对可追溯性的研究相比，代码生成对需求质量更敏感。

Conclusion: 需求异味密度增加通常会降低基于测试套件的函数正确性；不同类别的需求异味对生成代码的影响相似；高质量需求并不保证生成代码正确性，影响还依赖于所用的LLM和其他因素；与对可追溯性的影响相比，代码生成对需求质量更敏感。

Abstract: Software requirements are typically incorporated into prompts used in LLM-assisted software development. Recent work has shown that requirement smells can affect automated traceability between requirements and code, but empirical evidence on their effects in code generation remains limited. To address this gap, we build upon a prior study on automated traceability by reusing its dataset and requirement smell taxonomy, while extending it to evaluate the functional correctness of LLM-generated code. Using a benchmark consisting of requirements and corresponding system tests for four applications, we progressively introduced semantic, syntactic, and lexical smells into otherwise clear requirements and analyzed their influence on generated implementations. Our results suggest that increasing \textit{smell density} was generally associated with lower test-suite-based functional correctness, although non-smelly requirements could still produce faulty code. We also found that different smell categories had similar effects. These findings provide additional empirical evidence of the importance of requirement quality in LLM-assisted code generation, while showing that high-quality requirements alone do not guarantee correctness, as these depends on several factors, including the LLM. Compared with previous work, our results suggest that the impact of requirement smells depends on the software engineering task: whereas their effects on traceability were modest, code generation appears more sensitive. Overall, this work motivates further investigation into task-dependent quality effects in LLM-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [121] [Model-Based Retargeting to Many-Core CPS: Simulink-to-OpenCL Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29311)
*Ryuga Chiba,Hiroshi Fujimoto,Takuya Azumi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种保留工作流的重定向方法：利用GPU Coder从Simulink导出CUDA，再通过自定义框架把CUDA转为OpenCL，从而将数据并行Simulink应用部署到Kalray MPPA上，验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bridge the software portability gap between Model-Based Development (Simulink) and advanced many-core architectures for CPS by avoiding manual model changes while targeting non-CUDA many-core processors.

Method: Use MathWorks GPU Coder to extract data-parallel CUDA kernels from Simulink models; apply a custom translation framework to rewrite CUDA syntax, emulate CUDA APIs, and perform platform-specific argument packing to produce OpenCL host and device code; deploy and evaluate on the Kalray MPPA Coolidge2 using a Frenet-frame trajectory planner.

Result: Successful retargeting and deployment of a computationally intensive Frenet-frame trajectory planner on the Kalray MPPA Coolidge2, demonstrating the pipeline's feasibility for the evaluated workload and platform; specifics on performance/overheads are indicated but not detailed in the abstract.

Conclusion: The paper shows a feasible workflow-preserving retargeting pipeline that converts Simulink-generated CUDA code into OpenCL host/device code, enabling deployment of candidate-wise data-parallel Simulink applications on many-core processors without manual model rewriting.

Abstract: This paper addresses the software portability gap between Model-Based Development (MBD) and advanced many-core execution for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS). We present a workflow-preserving retargeting approach for Simulink-based CPS applications with candidate-wise data parallelism to OpenCL-based many-core processors. Rather than manually rewriting models for new platforms, our toolchain uses MathWorks GPU Coder to extract data-parallel CUDA code, which is then translated into OpenCL host and device code via a custom framework. The conversion handles syntax rewriting, API emulation, and platform-specific argument packing. We deployed this workflow for a computationally intensive Frenet-frame trajectory planner on the Kalray MPPA Coolidge2. The results demonstrate the feasibility of a workflow-preserving retargeting pipeline for the evaluated CPS workload and platform.

</details>


### [122] [Demystifying Agent Skills for Smart Contract Auditing: Design, Effectiveness, Behavioral Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29454)
*Cuifeng Gao,Juantao Zhong,Jiachi Chen,Shuai Wang,Daoyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 收集并评估83个智能合约审计技能：模型决定效果大小，技能触发是瓶颈；被触发时技能保持六阶段工作流但在表现上有差异，并开源了语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何设计和使用可复用的“技能”来增强LLM代理在专业领域（此处为智能合约审计）中的表现，并揭示技能设计与代理真实行为之间的关系与瓶颈。

Method: 作者从真实世界收集了83个智能合约审计技能，并在EVMBench基准上对七种代理-模型配置进行了系统评估，从技能设计特征、对漏洞检测的提升效果、对代理执行轨迹的影响三方面分析。

Result: 发现技能设计轻量且异质，覆盖漏洞类型广但分布不均；模型能力（如Codex/GPT-5.5）对检测性能带来最大增益（检测分数提升22.8%，获奖/捕获收益提升43.2%）；技能触发率低是限制效果的关键；触发后技能遵循共有六阶段审计流程，但在加载模式和对代理行为的影响上因配置不同而异。作者同时公开了技能语料与实验产物。

Conclusion: 本文表明：在智能合约安全审计这一任务中，面向“技能”（skills）的封装可改善大模型代理的审计效果，但改善幅度主要受底层模型能力驱动，而非代理外壳；技能触发（triggering）是主要瓶颈；被触发时，技能通常沿用同一六阶段审计工作流，但在加载和行为影响上表现差异化。

Abstract: LLM agents, notably Claude Code and OpenAI Codex, are emerging as versatile tools beyond coding agents only. These agents can be enhanced with skills---reusable artifacts that package domain knowledge, workflows, and tool-use instructions. To date, however, little is known about how such skills are designed or how they affect agent effectiveness and behavior in practice. In this paper, we investigate these questions in smart contract security auditing, a domain in which agents have shown substantial promise. We systematically collect 83 smart contract audit skills from the wild and evaluate them on EVMBench across seven agent--model configurations. Our study examines three dimensions: (i) the design characteristics of audit skills, including their structure, knowledge representations, workflows, and tool dependencies; (ii) their effectiveness in improving vulnerability detection; and (iii) their influence on agent execution trajectories. We find that audit skills are mostly lightweight but heterogeneous in design, covering a broad yet imbalanced range of vulnerability types. Their effectiveness is determined primarily by the model rather than the agent harness: Codex/GPT-5.5 achieves the largest gains, improving detection score by 22.8% and captured award by 43.2%. We further find that skill triggering is a key bottleneck. When triggered, skills preserve a shared six-stage audit workflow while exhibiting distinct loading patterns and differential effects on agent behavior across configurations. We release our skill corpus and artifacts to support future research.

</details>


### [123] [Judgment-Centred Software Engineering Education: A Post-Hype Review and Framework for AI-Augmented Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29473)
*Qusay H. Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了2023-2026年关于生成式AI在软件工程教育中的证据，认为GenAI能改善短期学习支持，但长期学习与维护风险取决于课程设计与证据保障；提出理解债务概念并给出AASEE框架以指导教学治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问题不再是是否允许学生生成代码，而是如何在为学生准备与越来越强大的AI系统负责任地共事时，保持并评估其人类理解能力。

Method: 对2023年至2026年9月23日的研究与实践进行结构化整合综述，并补充人工智能素养、技术债务与人机协作等既有工作。

Result: 提出并扩展了“理解债务”（comprehension debt）概念，提出AASEE框架的五个非线性整合层级和四个横向证据义务（解释、验证、修改、说明），并将委托与证据、治理与恢复机制相链接。

Conclusion: 论文得出条件性结论：生成式人工智能（GenAI）可以在解释、反馈、练习和短期任务完成方面提供改进，但学习效果依赖先验知识、支架、验证、任务设计和评估。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has moved from a disruptive novelty to a recurring part of software-development and computing-education workflows, while software agents are beginning to act across repositories, command lines, browsers, tests, and other tools. The educational problem is no longer whether students should be allowed to generate code, but whether software-engineering (SE) programs can preserve and assess human understanding while preparing students to work responsibly with increasingly capable AI systems. This paper presents a structured integrative review of research and practice from 2023 through 23 September 2026, supplemented by established work on AI literacy, technical debt, and human-AI collaboration. The evidence supports a conditional conclusion: GenAI can improve access to explanations, feedback, practice, and short-term task completion, but learning outcomes depend on prior knowledge, scaffolding, verification, task design, and assessment. We examine student help-seeking and authorship, faculty assessment and policy demands, and the wider SE risks created when generated artifacts persist across repositories, teams, architectures, and deployed systems. We extend the concept of comprehension debt: the deferred learning and maintenance cost that arises when AI-assisted production outpaces a learner's or team's ability to explain, test, modify, and justify the resulting software. We then refine the AI-Augmented Software Engineering Education (AASEE) framework into five non-linear integration levels and four cross-cutting evidence obligations: explain, verify, modify, and account. The framework links delegation to evidence, governance, and recovery mechanisms appropriate to its consequences.

</details>


### [124] [CodeGraph: Open-Taxonomy Knowledge Graph for Source Code with Wikidata Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29474)
*Federico Pennino,Andrea Gurioli,Stefano Zacchiroli,Maurizio Gabbrielli,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: A scalable pipeline using a code-focused LLM plus deterministic and learned Wikidata linking yields CodeGraph, the first large-scale open-taxonomy knowledge graph for source code, with cross-referenced concepts and a quantified precision estimate.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing repository-scale analysis is mostly syntactic/token-level; the work aims to extract higher-level engineering knowledge (algorithms, paradigms, patterns, domains) at repository scale and make it linkable to an open knowledge base (Wikidata).
